### fr 侧事实（执法点/缝面核实）
- PRD 4.5：fr-tools-builtin=第 17 节全部工具单 crate；无 fr-fs/fr-spill crate（spill 归 fr-compaction Phase 17）；fr-attachment（Q78-A）provide "attachments" 但现有面=request-image 管线+AttachmentRead trait，**无 durable store**（saveImage/imageLimits/AttachmentError 码族/AttachmentId 仅 fr-wire ids 有形）。
- fr-llm：ResolvedModelInfo/LlmModelInfo/ProviderInfo 面存在+plugin_shell provide 'llm'；resolve-model-info wire 方法待核（批 3）。
- fr-sandbox：escalation.rs 全套（ESCALATION_TARGETS/validate_escalation_args/sandbox_denial_marker/escalation_hint_marker/sandbox_permissions_description/approve_escalation）+roots.rs（writable_roots/canonical_path，dsh 同源共享推导）+path_boundary.rs——fs fence 消费即合。
- fr-shell subprocess face：describe0/spawn1/read2/wait3/terminate4/resolve_executable5（stdio per-stream collect/pipe/inherit+grace+signal 对接 caller-abort 面）。
- fr-wire：无 fs/spill-store/llm service face（16b 视裁决增）；tools face REGISTER_LISTENER=1/UNREGISTER=2（Q101′A 面在）。
- fr-tools：definition timeout_ms+concurrency_safe 双字段在（WireToolRegistration）+on_pre/post_execute 管线在（Q101′A stage 序）；fr-guard-timeout 消费 timeout_ms 建 deadline（协作超时走 exec signal，aborted→SEARCH_ABORTED 语义对齐）。
- fr-tools-builtin：serve dispatch 方法号 0-5+11-14+900；plugin_shell prompt_sections=常量注入（批 3 判例）；working_directory::FsStat seam（resolve+is_dir）=16b 迁移点；escalation 桥 bash 判例全同构。
- fr-system-prompt orders：TOOL_READ 1100/TOOL_WRITE 1200/TOOL_EDIT 1300/TOOL_GLOB 1400/TOOL_GREP 1500 已钉；无 TOOL_STR_REPLACE（dsh 同）。
- dougong-abi：require 三态 OK/UNDECLARED(-3)/INACTIVE(-4)——setup 期 probe 可作条件注册载体（INACTIVE=provider 未提交→skip 注册）；装配行序 provider 先于 consumer 为组合纪律。

### 设计钉（非分叉，源文/PRD 已裁）
1. fs 域=fr-tools-builtin in-crate 域模块（PRD 4.5 无 fr-fs；Q93-A 域服务归属；bundle 实证 fs-sandbox 单臂→fr 单一 sandboxed local backend，无裸 fs-local 臂）。fs-local（3396 行）批 1 全读后移植：resolve（最深存在祖先 realpath）/stat/version token/readText/streamText/readBytes/listDir/writeText（原子写+LF 归一+before diff basis+cap 双侧 exclusive 限）/editText（CAS 临界区）。
2. 观察策略=fr-tools-builtin in-crate 模块（PRD:434 属 fs 工具域）；write/edit 工具体直调 gate（同 crate 无跨插件瀑布，Q101′A 判例）；fs/observed 同步记录；owner=agent session 标识。
3. fence：write/edit 委托前 checkedTarget 同构（resolve 复用 fr-sandbox roots/path_boundary；拒绝文案两 mode 逐字；FS_SANDBOX_DENIED 保码）；escalation 全走 fr-sandbox 既有面（subject='operation'）。
4. ripgrep 解析序=FR_RIPGREP→PATH（fr-shell resolve_executable）→缺失 `grep 工具明确报错`（spec 既裁）；argv 前置 --no-config；打包期捆绑=release 工程不入 M1 代码。
5. glob/grep 经 fr-shell subprocess spawn 前台（collect caps/graceMs/exec signal 传播）；永不后台 job 无 shell 层。
6. timeoutMs 30000 声明+fr-guard-timeout 协作超时（既有面）；aborted→SEARCH_ABORTED。
7. spill 消费缝 trySaveFormattedResult 三 warn 臂逐字；provider 实体归 fr-compaction（Phase 17）。
8. schema 七工具全参数表经 generate-tool-catalog.py 抽取（Q95-A/Q97-A 判例）；section 文本 tool:read/write/edit/glob/grep 五件 extract_section_text 扩源（批 3 裁决 A 判例同构）；write/edit escalation 两字段=注册时变体（16a 判例）。
9. 方法号段（钉案）：read 20-24（execute/render/presentCall/presentResult/presentationMeta）、write 30-34、edit 40-44、read_image 50-52（execute/render/presentCall）、glob 60-64、grep 70-74、str_replace_editor 80-82（execute/render/presentCall）；describe 扩方法表。
10. config 键（kebab）：read/read-image/write/edit/read-limit/read-max-line-length/read-max-bytes/read-stream-min-size/sample-over-cap-glob-results（required 无默认，dsh bundle=false 同值装配）/glob-max-results/grep-max-matches/grep-max-line-bytes/search-meta-max-bytes/raw-output-max-bytes/grace-ms/stderr-max-bytes/timeout-ms/str-replace-editor opt-in 旋钮（默认 false）/max-output-chars。
11. read_image 注册门=setup 期 require probe（INACTIVE→skip=「无 ctx.attachments 不注册」）+执行体防御 re-check 臂（fail-closed 文案同 dsh）；装配行序 providers→fr-tools-builtin。
12. working_directory 迁移 FsStat→in-crate fs 域（resolve+stat 超集）；trait 收编删除。
13. isConcurrencySafe：read/read_image=true→parallel；write/edit/glob/grep/str_replace_editor 缺省→exclusive（spec 场景族既裁语义）。

- 批次计划（裁决后定案）：批 1=fr-wire 两 face（fs 14 法+spill-store）+fs-local 后端移植+观察策略 gate+fence；批 2=read/write/edit 三工具+working_directory 迁移；批 3=glob/grep+spill 消费+str_replace_editor+修场景④；批 4=read_image 全落（attachment store+llm 缝+路由头缝）；批 5=spec 绑定全族+七步流 4-7 收口+五门。

### 16b 批 1 落地（2026-10-29）：fs 域后端+观察策略+fence+两 wire face，workspace 1186 tests（1168→1186）
- **fr-wire 两 face**：`fs` face（SERVICE_NAME="fs"，方法号=fr-tools-builtin 工件 1000+ 段：describe1000/resolve1001/process_path1002/process_path_from_host_path1003/file_url1004/contains1005/stat1006/lstat1007/read_text1008/stream_text1009/read_bytes1010/read_byte_range1011/list_dir1012/write_text1013/edit_text1014/watch1015——单 call 导出全工件方法号唯一判例，工具段 20+ 不冲突）；`spill_store` face（"spill-store"，save_text=0）。fr 自造 wire 形 snake_case（16a 判例）。
- **fs_types.rs**：FsErrorCode 13 码（serde 显式 rename——rename_all 无法从 PascalCase 生成下划线形式，serve 钉测逮住）+FsError{code,message}+From<io::Error>（IoError 臂）+FsTarget/FsInfo/FsPathInfo/FsDirEntry/FsObservation(present/absent tag=kind)/FsWriteIntent(create-if-absent/replace-if-version)/FsWriteOutcome/FsEditRequest/FsEditOutcome/WriteOperation。
- **fsio.rs**（836 行 TS→Rust 全移植）：version_of（unix dev:ino:size:mtime_nsec:ctime_nsec；windows FILETIME 三元——token 不透明内部）；local_display_path 双平台臂（POSIX `..` 段保物理拼写/其余 lexical_resolve；Windows 恒 lexical）；resolve_local_target（strict canonicalize→ENOENT 祖先走查补 missing 后缀；ENOTDIR→FS_NOT_FOUND 逐字；missing 含 `..`→crossing 逐字）；probe/probe_no_follow（ENOENT/ENOTDIR→None）；list_directory（名字序=codepoint 序——dsh localeCompare 非 ASCII 分歧已记 19.2 候选）；read_whole_text（8KiB NUL 采样+strict UTF-8）；read_whole_bytes（stat 短路+cap+1 读）；read_byte_window；StreamWholeText（拉式迭代器：64KiB chunk+4 字节 carry 跨界 UTF-8+采样窗）；write_file_atomic（staging `.base.pid.uuid.tmpdir` 0700+wx 0600+sync+mode 恢复+POSIX rename/createIfAbsent hard_link EEXIST→FS_NOT_OBSERVED 分类；windows ReplaceFileW FFI+ENOENT 落 rename 回退）；read_for_edit（全 buffer NUL 扫+LF 归一+CRLF 探测 4096 字符样本）；apply_literal_edit（old/new 归一+三拒绝文案逐字）；read_text_for_diff（描述符界 bound+size 变化/NUL/非法 UTF-8→None+**LF 归一**——钉测逮住首漏）。
- **fence.rs**：checked_target（danger 直通/read-only 逐字拒/workspace-write fresh 重规范化+writable_roots 逐根 contains（lexical 等径快路径）/拒绝文案两 mode 逐字保码）；fr-sandbox 只作 pub API 消费（writable_roots/canonical_path/seam::SandboxMode::parse），不动其源码。
- **observation.rs**：ObservedStateGate（owner=session id；write_intent/edit_intent/observe/clear；五钉测含 owner 域隔离）。
- **fs_domain.rs**：FsDomainConfig（cwd 默认 process.cwd/diff_basis_max_bytes 10MiB）；per-targetKey Arc 锁表（进程生存期）；15 法（resolve/process_path/process_path_from_host_path（lexical 规范化）/file_url（pathToFileURL 百分号编码集）/contains/stat/lstat/read_text/stream_text（拉迭代器）/read_bytes/read_byte_range/list_dir/write_text（fence 先行→锁内 probe→三守卫→diff basis→原子写→versionAfterWrite missing: sentinel）/edit_text（锁内 stale 前置→readForEdit→literal→CRLF 恢复写回））；watch=基类拒绝（`Filesystem watching is not supported by this provider.`——fs-local chokidar 覆写随首消费者落，Q105′A 序）；serve 两段式（`{value}|{error}` Ok-body 协议=16a 判例，decode/未知方法才 wire Err；13 钉测：resolve 双腿/read 拒绝族/write 守卫族/CRLF diff basis/edit 族/fence 三 mode+tmp/serve wire 往返+错误体形状）。
- **plugin_shell**：descriptor provide+=["fs"]；BuiltinState.fs=Arc<FsDomain>（WireStandingPolicy=sandbox-policy resolve 缝）；call 出口 1000-1015 段分流 fs.serve。
- **working_directory**：StdFsStat 收编迁移（is_directory→fsio::probe 单实现；16a 留的迁移点兑现）。
- **五门全绿**：fmt/clippy workspace -D warnings 0/tests 1186+0（+18=observation 5+fs_domain 13）/doc -D warnings/Windows msvc check fr-tools-builtin 零警告（has_parent_segment cfg(unix) 门）。
- 过程注记：本批 3 起 Write/heredoc 生成退化全部当场截停（fs_types 两度+补丁期 is::none 签名自检逮住）——小片段+逐段校验纪律再次生效；三处真缺陷由钉测逮住自纠（diff basis 归一/fence 测试前提（/tmp 本为可写根，dsh 同语义）/错误体双编码）。

## 16b 批 3 设计钉（2026-10-30，真源全读+执法点实证后钉存）

### 真源补充全读（批 3 面）
- dsh tool-fs-search 五文件全读（search-core 413/glob 368/grep 366/presentation 205/direct-call 28）+tool-str-replace-editor/index.ts 532 行全读；tool-catalog.md glob/grep/str_replace_editor 三节（json fence 全在）。
- fr 侧执法点实证：fr-tools service.rs 监听协议（RegisterListenerRequest{stage,endpoint}/PostEnter→Delegate→PostExit{decision}→Final）；WireToolRegistration.execute 载 AbortWatcher（50ms 轮询 fused signal→jobs 1008 kill，reason "tool call aborted"）；fr-guard-timeout 在 process/插件双形态都是 execute 包裹：own timer fired→替换 tool_timeout_result（TOOL_TIMEOUT，对齐 dsh guard/timeout-policy/src/index.ts:73-75 逐字语义）；fr-shell subprocess face：spawn(WireSpawnSpec{argv,cwd,stdio,grace_ms,env})→{handle}/wait(handle,timeout_ms)→{settled}|outcome{exit_code,signal}/terminate/resolve_executable（PATH）；**subprocess 表无 tool-call 绑定、spawn 信号参恒 None**；jobs kill_by_tool_call 只扫 jobs store。
- dsh registry abort 语义实证（core/tools/src/index.ts dispatchToolBody）：`isAborted→toolAbortedResult` 只在成功臂（createSuccessResult 之后）；体抛错走 catch→toolErrorResult 原样浮出（含 abort 竞态）。timeout 腿模型最终看到 TOOL_TIMEOUT（guard 替换，含体的 SEARCH_ABORTED）；caller-abort 腿体的 SEARCH_ABORTED 逐字浮出（registry 的 callerCancelled 替换仅及非 isError）。
- fr 现状偏差实证：registry.rs dispatch_tool_body 在体返回后**无条件** `fused.is_aborted()→tool_aborted_result`（含体错误）——与 dsh 成功臂限定不符，caller-abort 时体的 SEARCH_ABORTED 会被通用 abort 文本吞掉。

### 设计钉（非分叉）
1. 模块：search_core（SEARCH_* 四码词表+spawn/等待切片+head 保留+workdir 相对化+spill 三 warn 臂）/search_glob/search_grep/search_presentation（SearchMeta 投影+capMetaBytes 尾部丢弃保单件+防御收窄零结果合法）/sre_tools（四命令体+MutationPolicy 简形）。
2. 方法号（批 0 钉 9 兑现+补）：glob 60-64/grep 70-74/sre 80-82（execute/render/presentCall；sre 无 presentationMeta/presentResult——dsh 同）；spill 监听两法 glob=65/grep=75（工具段延续判例）；describe_methods 26→39。
3. spawn 经 fr-shell subprocess face（Seams 增 subprocess_spawn/read/wait/terminate/resolve_executable 五法）；DESCRIPTOR inject += "subprocess"（dylib 能力基础；未声明=STATUS_UNDECLARED）；spill-store 不入 inject（机会式 require——UNDECLARED/INACTIVE=dsh ctx.get undefined 同型=臂 2）。
4. rg 解析序=FR_RIPGREP（绝对+可执行才用）→resolve_executable("rg")（PATH）→SEARCH_FAILED 逐字 dsh 启动失败文案 `could not start its search command (ripgrep launch failed)`；argv 前置 --no-config。
5. 体等待=wait 切片循环（200ms，jobs 判例）；settle 后 readFrom(0) 双流；completeStdout 双 OVERFLOW 文案逐字；classifyRunFailure（regex parse error|error parsing glob→INVALID_PATTERN）。entry 信号预检结构性缺席（F3 signal 不过线；前置由 dispatch ABORTED_BEFORE_DISPATCH 覆盖，slice-7 判例族）。
6. spill 三 warn 臂逐字（no session owner/no ctx.spillStore backend/saveText failed）；owner=agent；source {kind:'tool',toolName,callId,label:'result'}；成功不转 isError；M1 无 provider=臂 2 常态如实档载；测试真实文件后端供缝（Q16b-2A）。
7. post-execute CPS：acceptedDirectCallValue port=accept+content/value 均空+parent 无+name 匹配+!isError+值形状守卫；dsh ctx.tools.get 同一性检查结构性缺席（registry 身份不过线；模块 doc veracity 注记）。
8. glob/grep 描述组合：catalog 冻结 sampled=true 文案≠baked false 注册变体（恰一短语差，pwsh appendix 判例）；脚本从 glob.ts/grep.ts 源段抽取 head/overcap 两变体/tail+maxResults/maxMatches 插值；冻结钉测 sampled=true 组合对 catalog 逐字。section：tool:glob 1400 单段；tool:grep 1500=base+(read 注册?tail) 段抽取条件组合（write 判例）。
9. caps 烘焙（bundle 判例）：glob_max_results 100/grep_max_matches 250/grep_max_line_bytes 2000/search_meta_max_bytes 65536/raw_output_max_bytes 20971520/grace_ms 3000/stderr_max_bytes 65536/timeout_ms 30000/sample_over_cap_glob_results=false（bundle 实证）；isConcurrencySafe 缺省（exclusive）；timeoutMs=30000 注册携带。
10. sre 逐字序（dsh index.ts）：四命令臂；edit_intent 槽在 remap 链外（dsh waterfall 序）；ambiguous 行号表/`%6d` 双空格/view_range 四拒绝/insert_line 界文案/maybeTruncate（UTF-16 单位预算+不劈代理对）；目录两层列唯独（隐藏/node_modules/__pycache__ 排除+`d\troot` 头行+codepoint 序）；MutationPolicy 简形=裸 denial marker 无 hint。
11. sre 注册门=baked opt-in 旋钮 false→M1 不注册（spec「替代编辑器默认不装配」既裁）；体经 serve 全臂钉测；schema required=[command,path]+五键 oneOf(type|null)；description=DEFAULT_DESCRIPTION catalog 逐字。
12. catalog 脚本扩源：TOOLS 增 glob(pattern,path)/grep(pattern,path,include)/str_replace_editor 七键；TOOL_SOURCES 增 glob.ts/grep.ts；sre section 旗标=none；emit 侧 sre 跳过 section 抽取。
13. spec 修场景④（Q16b-4A 已裁）：「str_replace_editor 说明 section 注册」经 authoring 改「description 逐字+presentCall 四臂呈现」；改后 parse+lint 0.7 复验。
14. freeze 钉测扩 glob/grep/sre 三注册（sre 表即 catalog 原样、无 escalation 对）。

### 分叉裁决（2026-10-30 用户确认，Q16b-3A/3B 双 A）
- **Q16b-3A** 扩 Q16a-6 载体到 subprocess face：spawn wire spec 增 `tool_call` 绑定；jobs 1008 同扫 subprocess 表（terminate+记 kill 原因）；wait 回执带 kill 原因；search 体映射 dsh SEARCH_ABORTED 逐字文案（超时与 caller-abort 两腿全对齐）。
- **Q16b-3B** 修码对齐 dsh：fr registry 中止替换臂移入成功臂（仅成功结果被替换，体错误原样浮出），钉测加验。

### 设计钉（非分叉）
1. 模块：fs_read_render（read-render.ts+code-language 表）/fs_diff（jsdiff 9.0.0 移植）/fs_sandbox（sandbox.ts 控制器 subject='operation'）/fs_tools（read/write/edit 体+remediate+mutation_result）。
2. 方法号（钉案批 0 钉 9 兑现）：read 20-24/write 30-34/edit 40-44（execute/render/presentCall/presentResult/presentationMeta）；serve 第三带 20..=44 在 BuiltinState（fs 域 1000-1015 不动）；describe_methods 11→26。
3. caps 烘焙（ExecutorConfigs::bundle 判例）：read_limit 2000/read_max_line_length 2000/read_max_bytes 51200/read_stream_min_size 10485760；read/write/edit/read_image 开关默认全 true（M1 组合全注册；catalog 冻结文 "Defaults to 2000." 同源）。
4. isConcurrencySafe：read=Some(true)，write/edit 缺省。
5. escalation 桥：FsSandboxController——escalationModes=fs.sandbox_mode() 有无；schemaFields description=sandbox_permissions_description("operation")+justification fs 文（"this exact file operation"）；resolve_policy=validate→standing resolve→双参缺席早返→modes 空 plain（fs 词 "no sandboxing filesystem to escalate"）→approve_escalation(subject="operation")→mode 盖章合并；map_error 仅 FS_SANDBOX_DENIED→denialMarker+\n+hintMarker('operation') 保码。注册面 escalation 对公布条件=fs.sandbox_mode().is_some()（=16a 既有 escalation 非空旗标）。
6. 观察门：BuiltinState.gate=ObservedStateGate；read 成功 observe(present)/缺席 observe(absent)+拒；write：intent（gate.write_intent，try 外）→mutate；edit：intent（gate.edit_intent，try 内=FS_NOT_OBSERVED 也走 remediate）→mutate；mutation_result=observe(present,version)+剥 version+path=processPath。owner=agent。
7. 错误序：mapError 先、remediate 后（dsh 同序）；FS_NOT_OBSERVED→`cannot modify "P": file has not been read — read the file, then retry`；FS_STALE_VERSION→原文本+` — re-read the file, then retry`；FsError=plain（dsh 非 HarnessError 不走 code 路由）。
8. section 文本：tool:read 1100 静态；tool:write 1200=base+(edit 注册?tail)+'.（脚本抽 3 段，剥 ctx.tools.get 字面量，弃空字面量）；tool:edit 1300 静态；注册条件=对应工具注册（16a 判例）。
9. catalog 脚本扩源：TOOLS 增 read/write/edit（per-tool 参数键表 read: file_path/offset/limit、write: file_path/content、edit: file_path/old_string/new_string/replace_all）+TOOL_SOURCES 三路径+extract_section_text_segments 变体；输出 schema=TS defineTool 逐字（bash 判例手建）。
10. read 窗口：build_window 逐字（lineBufferCap=maxLineLength+1/stripCarriageReturn/行字节=UTF8+非首行1/finish offset 越界 FS_NOT_FOUND 文案/formatReadOutput 信封+三 footer）；lang 表=扩展名→短 id 单表静态匹配（双分隔符/末点/leading dot 即分隔/toLowerCase）。
11. diff 移植：myers（bestPath 链组件+tie-break `!canRemove || (canAdd && removePath.oldPos < addPath.oldPos)`→add 否则 remove+min/maxDiagonalToConsider 剪枝+extractCommon）；tokenize（split 保留分隔符归并+尾空弃+removeEmpty）；buildPatch（context=3、重叠判定 lines.len<=2*context && i<diff.len-2、contextLines ' ' 前缀、hunk 头四元组、step2 尾 \n 剥+No-newline 标记插入）；computeHunkDiffs 跳 '\\' 行、纯插入 oldText:null。
12. presentResult：read=meta 收窄（语义校验逐字）+信封正则体抽取→card read；write=diffsFromMeta ?? args 兜底（meta 无/空 diff 均兜底）；edit=diffsFromMeta 缺→undefined；write/edit isError→undefined。
13. working_directory 收尾：FsStat trait+StdFsStat 删除；is_directory=直调 fsio::probe；测试换真实临时目录（fs_domain Scratch 判例）。
14. regex 入 fr-tools-builtin 依赖（read presentResult 信封解析）；DESCRIPTOR 不动（gate/fs in-crate）。

## 16b 批 4 设计钉（2026-10-30，真源全读+缝点执法核证后实施前钉存）
- 真源全读：tool-fs read-image.ts（350 行全量）+tool-fs index.ts（注册门=readImage 配置门×ctx.inject(['attachments']) 条件注册；read_image 无 prompt section）+attachment-local {store 458,index 291,file-store 181,normalization 137,image 133,request-image 210（fr 管线已移植）,compression-limiter 52,encoding 83（已移植）,sharp 7}+attachment 包 {error 86,request-projection 62}。fr 侧核证：fr-attachment 现有面=pipeline 1001 行（request-image 面+detect/encode/cache）+AttachmentRead 缝+describe-only dylib；fs_domain.read_bytes 带 cap 语义批 1 已钉（TooLarge 文案）；fr-wire 已有 Image/FileAttachmentRef+ImageMediaType 四值；fr-session Session::request_header() 折叠增量在（&mut）；fr-llm generation 已有 WireModelInfo wire 形+GenerationService sources 注册表（空，production 归 runtime-registry）；dougong STATUS_INACTIVE=-4=宿主 require 对未 commit provider fail-closed→setup 探针成立。
- **Q16b-1A 落地切面**：read_image 工具体归 fr-tools-builtin 新模块 fs_read_image.rs（dsh read-image.ts 逐字双胞胎）；durable store 面（saveImage/imageLimits/码族）归 fr-attachment rlib 新五模块：limits.rs（ImageAttachmentLimits+AttachmentConfig+DEFAULT_* 九常量+校验）/admission.rs（admission 侧 detect 全事实+limit 门+EXIF 取向）/normalize.rs（NormalizationPolicy+can_pass_through+normalize_image）/store.rs（prepare/commit/save/read+publish 机制族+sync_directory/ensure_durable_* 家族）/file_store.rs（verbatim 文件族+file_leaf_name）+local.rs（LocalAttachmentStore 组合：root=home/attachments/v1+cacheRoot+limiter+request 单飞 SharedRequest 同步移植）。
- **码族归置**：pipeline 四码常量原位不动；新增 error.rs 声明全族（IMAGE_ADMISSION 九+INVALID_FILE_BASE64/ATTACHMENT_CORRUPT/ATTACHMENT_NOT_FOUND/ATTACHMENT_READ_FAILED/ATTACHMENT_PROJECTION_UNSUPPORTED/ATTACHMENT_FILES_UNSUPPORTED）+is_image_admission_code/is_attachment_error_code 成员函数（dsh error.ts Set 语义）。AttachmentError 结构复用 pipeline 型（message+code &'static str）。
- **sharp→image crate 取向裁决**：fr detect_image（管线）保持 stored 轴+子集事实不动；admission 侧独立 AdmissionDetect（全事实：mediaType/dims 感知轴/animated/carriesMetadata/depth 词/alpha/space/orientation）。感知轴=image crate orientation()（JPEG/WebP decoder 实装；PNG eXIf 手析 TIFF IFD tag 274）5-8 转置；animated=PNG acTL（PngDecoder::is_apng 同源）+WebP ANIM/ANMF（RIFF 走查）+GIF 免检（canPassThrough 先 mediaType 短路）；carriesMetadata=sharp 词表对勘：PNG eXIf|iCCP|tEXt|iTXt|zTXt（gAMA/cHRM/sRGB/pHYs/tIME 等非 metadata）+JPEG APP1(Exif/XMP)|APP2 ICC|APP13 8BIM|COM+WebP EXIF|XMP|ICCP chunk。normalize 管线 rotate()=DynamicImage::apply_orientation 物理旋转→入库光栅恒直立→request-image 路径 stored 轴恒=感知轴（自洽）。
- **depth 词**：admission 侧 ColorType 映射 sharp 词：8-bit 族→uchar、16-bit 族（L16/La16/Rgb16/Rgba16）→ushort、F32 族→float；normalize 失败文案源描述 `The ${depth} PNG could not be converted...`（ushort→"16-bit PNG"，read_image 六臂正则 /16-bit PNG/i 的输入源）。
- **request_image_dimensions**（request-projection.ts 纯几何）新移植进 admission.rs（initial_dimensions 消费；while 收缩钉测锚）。
- **file-store 流面适配**：dsh saveFileStream/readFileStream=AsyncIterable；fr 同步面=chunks 切片输入 save_file_stream_verbatim(root,chunks,name,signal 检查点)+read_file_verbatim 返回 Vec<u8>（byte+digest 终验）——同步适配文档化（M1 无异步消费者，Q65 家族判例）。
- **fr-wire 增量**：services::attachments {SERVICE,DESCRIBE=1,SAVE_IMAGE=2,IMAGE_LIMITS=3}；services::llm {SERVICE,DESCRIBE=1,PREPARE_CALL=2,BEGIN=3,DRAIN=4,ABORT=5,RESOLVE_MODEL_INFO=6}（与 fr-llm generation 方法号对齐）；session_persistence METHOD_REQUEST_HEADER=10（reply {"header":EpochHeader|null} 全保真，消费侧读 .config）；新增 src/base64.rs（标准字母表+padding，encode/decode+钉测；save_image wire 载荷编码，双 crate 共用）。
- **fr-llm resolve-model-info 缝**：GenerationSource::resolve_model 默认实现=默认 CallConfig 走 prepare().model（镜像 LlmAdapter::prepare_call 默认形）；GenerationService::resolve_model_info(provider,model)->WireModelInfo（source_of NO_ADAPTER 文案逐字）；serve 臂 RESOLVE_MODEL_INFO=6+plugin_shell 路由；失败体=llmError 文档（ShellFailure::Llm 既有形）。
- **fr-session 路由头缝**：service.rs METHOD_REQUEST_HEADER=10+service_request_header（require_id+store.get_mut+Session::request_header 折叠→{"header":header|null}）；所有者检查与 session_read 同款 `session "X" is not live in this store`。
- **fr-tools-builtin**：方法号 50=READ_IMAGE_EXECUTE/51=RENDER/52=PRESENT_CALL/53=PRESENTATION_META（钉 50-52 增 53：dsh read_image 有 presentationMeta（只存 path 注释逐字）而无 presentResult——钉案笔误修正，批注载）。band 路由 plugin_shell call 增 50..=53 臂。Seams 增三子缝 trait：AttachmentSeam{image_limits,save_image}+LlmSeam{resolve_model_info}+RouteHeaderSeam{request_header}（Seams::attachment()/llm()/route_header() 访问器；WireSeams 实装 over HostLink；测试脚本化）。descriptor inject+="attachments","llm"。
- **read_image 注册门**：setup 期 BuiltinState 装配后 probe link.require(attachments,DESCRIBE)——Ok→注册行（isConcurrencySafe=true；presentResult 端点缺省同 str_replace_editor 形）；Err(含 STATUS_INACTIVE/-4 与 UNDECLARED)→skip。执行体防御 re-check：AttachmentSeam require 失败→`cannot read "P" as an image: no attachment service is mounted`（fail-closed 同 dsh 直呼语境）。
- **路由头 fallback 臂裁决（结构不可达注记）**：dsh `routed?.provider ?? exec.agent?.options.provider`——fr M1 执行帧无 agent options 面；结构性论证：模型签发的 read_image 调用必然先有一次模型请求→session 日志必有 request/header 事件→routed 恒在；options fallback 臂=防御死臂注记（agent=None 嵌套派发→routed None→"could not be resolved" 拒绝，与 dsh agent?. 可选链同构）。LlmSeam require 失败体含 llmError→原样浮出（UNKNOWN_MODEL 族）；否则→route-unresolved 文案（dsh llm undefined 同臂）。
- **save_image wire 编码**：{data:&lt;base64&gt;,mediaType,name?}→ref JSON（ImageAttachmentRef serde camelCase 既有）；image_limits reply={maxImageBytes,maxImagesPerMessage,maxMessageImageBytes,maxImagePixels,maxImageDimension,mediaTypes:[四值]}。byteCap=min(maxImageBytes,maxMessageImageBytes) 消费侧算。
- **品牌适配两处（Q76-B 判例）**：attachment-local 配置校验文案 `attachment-local: imageCompressionConcurrency must be an integer from 1 through 8`→`fr-attachment: image-compression-concurrency must be an integer from 1 through 8`（键名随 fr kebab 规约）；dsh-home→FR_HOME/`~/.fr`（shell_env::resolve_home 判例）；OWNED_FILE_PREFIX 'dsh-' 品牌点不属本批（upload-index 属 deepseek file-store，Phase 18 前置）。
- **catalog**：TOOLS["read_image"]=("READ_IMAGE",[("file_path","FILE_PATH_TEXT")],"none")+重生成（FR_DSH_SOURCE 必需）；READ_IMAGE_DESCRIPTION 从 tool-catalog.md :1065 抽取。
- **spec**：TOOLS Boundaries 扩 crates/fr-attachment/**+crates/fr-llm/**（Q16b-1A 合同变更随裁决生效）；read_image 场景绑定批 5 全族收口。
- 执行序：fr-wire（base64+三 face）→fr-attachment（七模块+shell）→fr-llm 缝→fr-session 缝→fr-tools-builtin（seams+fs_read_image+band+schema+catalog+注册门）→spec Boundaries→五门+合约三开关。

## 16b 批 4 落地注记（2026-10-30）
- 批 4 全落形态与设计钉一致；两处实施期补钉：①方法号 50-52 扩 50-53（dsh read_image 有 presentationMeta 无 presentResult，钉案笔误修正，批注载）；②钉测逮真 bug 一起：fr-wire base64 decode 原为 lenient 尾窗（"Zg=" 判 Ok），unwrap_err 钉测逮住后改为严格四字组（RFC 4648 canonical）。
- save_image wire 错误体＝{"attachmentError":{message,code}} STATUS_FAILED 文档；body 侧 save_failure 剥 require 传输前缀（"failed with host status N: "）后解析文档。
- 未挂载判定：require 错误文本含 "host status"（STATUS_INACTIVE=-4/UNDECLARED 族）→ dsh `ctx.get` undefined 同臂（"no attachment service is mounted"/route-unresolved）；llm 服务侧拒绝（UNKNOWN_MODEL 族 llmError 文档）原样浮出。
- 路由头 fallback 论证落实：模型签发的 read_image 调用必先有一次模型请求→request/header 恒已入日志→dsh `?? agent.options` 臂=防御死臂（fr M1 帧无 agent options 面，注记于 fs_read_image doc）。
- 批 5 入口：read_image 场景族绑定（registration gate/unmounted/route gate 双拒/六 save 臂/extension-less sniff/downscaled envelope/render image 块/presentCall 卡/presentationMeta path-only/isConcurrencySafe），接批 3 已绑族之后续绑。

### 16c 域深读（2026-10-30）：todo + goal + jobs 工具真源

**tool-todo（212 行全读）**：
- inject=['tools','sessionProjections']；Config.allowParallelInProgress **required 无默认**（z.boolean().required()）
- description 三段拼装：HEAD='Record and update a task list to plan multi-step work and show progress; skip it for trivial single-step tasks. Add one todo per concrete step before you start. ' + (allowParallel ? 'While work remains, keep the todos being worked on `in_progress`, several only when work runs in parallel. ' : 'While work remains, keep exactly one todo `in_progress`. ') + 'Mark each todo `completed` as soon as it is done.'
- toTodoList 校验（schema 后工具体层）：trim 空→`invalid todo: \`content\` must be a non-empty string`；重复→`invalid todos: duplicate content ${JSON.stringify(content)}`；!allowParallel 且 in_progress>1→`invalid todos: at most one task may be in_progress (got ${active})`
- execute：exec.agent 缺席→`todo_write requires an owning agent session`；agent.session.append('todo/write',{todos})；返回 {todos:[{content,status}],counts:{pending,inProgress,completed}}（wire 键 inProgress=camel 协议豁免）
- render：`Updated todo list: ${pending} pending, ${inProgress} in progress, ${completed} completed.`
- presentCall：{card:'generic',title:'Update todo list',kind:'other',rawInput:args.todos}
- projection：key 'todos'，state TodoItem[]|null，stateVersion 2；apply：todo/write→data.todos；turn/start→null（turn/end 不清=完成清单仍可见）；init null；wire view=state

**tool-jobs（416 行全读）**：
- inject=['tools','jobs','systemPrompt']；Config：waitTimeoutMs 默认 30_000/maxWaitTimeoutMs 默认 600_000/completionDelivery 'quiet'|'wakeup' 默认 'wakeup'/maxConsecutiveWakes 可选 min1
- 装配校验：`tool-jobs: waitTimeoutMs (${waitDefault}) exceeds maxWaitTimeoutMs (${waitCap})`；`tool-jobs: maxConsecutiveWakes (${N}) must be a whole number of turns`
- ctx.jobs.attachController('tool-jobs')（生产者门：不装 tool-jobs 则 bash 后台不可用）
- section 'tool:jobs' order=TOOL_JOBS，text 逐字（254 行：'Track every background job id you start. You are notified in-session when a job finishes — do not busy-poll or sleep on one; keep working on independent steps and do not duplicate a running job\'s work. Before giving a final answer, collect every still-relevant job with job_output (set wait: true only when you are genuinely blocked on it), and job_kill jobs that stopped mattering.'）
- 完成通知机（agent-loop 依赖）：jobs.events.subscribe({owners:'scope'})；settled 事件且非 killedByModel/非 awaited/非 teardown/owner 在场→createUserMessage source {kind:'tool-jobs',form:'notice',summary=boundContextSummary(`${kind} ${label} ${statusLine}`)}；wakeup+owner idle→followup（wakeBudget 内，spentWakes WeakMap 按 Agent，user 源 inbox/claimed 重置）否则 inject
- fitCompletionNotice 截断梯（outputLimitBytes 帽）：完整→`${prefix}${omitted}${action}`→`${prefix}${action}`→`background job ended; use job_list for id.`→retainHead('job_list',maxBytes)；prefix=`background job ${job.id}`，detail=` (${kind}: ${label}) finished ${statusLine}`，complete=prefix+detail+'. Read its output with job_output.'，action='\nDone; job_output.'，omitted='\n[notice truncated]'
- PUBLIC_JOB_SCHEMA：{id req/kind req/label req/status req enum [running,stopping,completed,killed,failed]/detail?/startedAt req int/finishedAt? int} additionalProperties:false
- job_output：params job_id req/wait bool/timeout_ms number（无 required）；execute：wait=true→jobs.wait(id, min(timeout??waitDefault, waitCap), agent?.id, signal)；readBody=read.chunks renderModelDelta+result（delta 非空且非 \n 尾则补 \n 接缝）；validateJobId 空→`invalid job_id: expected a non-empty string, got ${JSON.stringify(v)}`；output {text req, job req}；render：body（空→'(no new output)'）+（非 \n 尾补 \n）+statusLine(job)
- job_list：params {}；jobs.list(agent?.id)→publicJob[]；render：空→'(no background jobs)'；否则 `${id} [${kind}] ${status} — ${label}` join '\n'
- job_kill：params job_id req/reason 可选（'Optional short reason, recorded in the log and forwarded to the job.'）；execute：jobs.kill(id, agent?.id, reason)→'requested'|'already-finished'；requested→killedByModel.add；publicJob(jobs.get(id, agent?.id))；output {outcome req enum ['cancellation-requested','already-finished'], job req}；render：already→`job ${id} had already finished ${statusLine}` 否则 `requested cancellation of job ${id}`
- finalizeContent：tools/pre-execute prepend 钩记 visibleOutputLimit（job_output/job_kill 按 job_id 查 list 得 outputLimitBytes）；job_output 非 error 且默认渲染完整→fitWithSuffix(body, `\n${statusLine}`, maxBytes, '\n[output truncated]')；否则通用 boundSingleText '\n[result truncated]'
- presentCall：`Read output from background job ${job_id}` read rawInput=job_id / `List background jobs` read / `Kill background job ${job_id}` execute rawInput=job_id

**goal 域（types 152+domain 116+fold 349+index 653+runtime 30+client 10 全读）**：
- 词汇：GoalId（branded 串）；GoalRef{id,revision}；GoalPhase active|paused|blocked|complete；GoalBlockReason{code lower-kebab 正则 ^[a-z][a-z0-9]*(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$, message 非空 trim 归一}；GoalSnapshot extends GoalRef{objective,phase,blockedReason?,maxGoalRounds}；GoalActivation armed|disarmed（进程本地不持久）；GoalView=GoalSnapshot+roundsStarted+createdAt+updatedAt+activation；GoalProjection{goal,roundsStarted,createdAt,updatedAt}；GoalProjectionState{current,seenGoalIds[],failure}
- GoalOperation create|edit|pause|resume|complete|block|clear；goal/change 会话事件 version=1；snapshot 形 {kind,version,operation,goal,roundsStarted,createdAt,updatedAt} 恰七键；clear 形 {kind,version,operation:'clear',cleared:GoalRef,clearedAt} 恰五键
- fold 规则（全 strict）：snapshot 解码逐字段（revision/maxGoalRounds 正 safe int、objective 非空 trim 归一、phase 白名单、blocked 相恰六键否则恰五键、blockedReason 恰 code,message 两键）；updateDateAt<createdAt→fail；非 create 变更要求恰下一 revision+计数/时间戳保持；edit 不得改 phase/blockedReason；pause active→paused；resume active|paused|blocked→active 且 roundsStarted<maxGoalRounds；complete 非 complete→complete；block active→blocked；clear 要求 current 在场+恰下一 revision+clearedAt>=updatedAt，清空全部并 lastRef=tombstone
- create fold 规则：revision===1+phase active+roundsStarted 0+(无 current 或 current.phase==='complete')+id 未复用（seenGoalIds 拒绝复用）
- user/message source.kind==='goal' 事件推进 roundsStarted：要求恰 next round 且 goalId/revision 匹配 current active 且 round<=maxGoalRounds，否则 `goal round at session event ${seq} is not the next admitted round of the active goal`
- GoalService inject=['agents','sessionProjections']；Config defaultMaxGoalRounds 默认 256（resolveMaxGoalRounds 正 safe int）
- 投影注册：key 'goal' stateVersion **6**；init {current:null,seenGoalIds:[],failure:null}；apply=applyGoalProjection（failure 非空即短路；仅 goal/change 与 source.kind==='goal' 的 user/message 触发；折叠异常→failure=`goal replay failed at session event ${seq}: ${msg}` 不上抛）；wire view=state.current（GoalProjection|null）
- 服务 API（全部先 assertLive：ctx.agents.get(agent.id)!==agent→GOAL_AGENT_NOT_LIVE `agent "${id}" is not live in this registry`）：
  - get(agent)→GoalView|undefined
  - create(agent,{objective,maxGoalRounds?})：resolveObjective（trim 非空→GOAL_INVALID_OBJECTIVE 'goal objective must be a non-empty string'）；maxGoalRounds??256（GOAL_INVALID_MAX_ROUNDS 'maxGoalRounds must be a positive safe integer'）；current 存在且 phase!=='complete'→GOAL_ALREADY_EXISTS `goal "${id}" already exists with phase "${phase}"`；id=`goal-${randomUUID()}` revision1 phase active；armed
  - edit(agent,ref,{objective?,maxGoalRounds?})：两缺→GOAL_INVALID_EDIT 'goal edit requires objective and/or maxGoalRounds'；保持 phase/blockedReason；revision+1
  - pause：active→paused disarmed；resume：active|paused|blocked→active armed，active 且已 armed→GOAL_INVALID_TRANSITION `goal "${id}" is already active and armed`，roundsStarted>=cap→`goal "${id}" exhausted ${cap} goal rounds; increase maxGoalRounds before resuming`；complete：active|paused|blocked→complete disarmed；block：active→blocked+resolveBlockReason（code lower-kebab+message 非空→GOAL_INVALID_BLOCK_REASON 'goal block reason requires a lower-kebab-case code and a non-empty message'）
  - clear(agent,ref)→tombstone GoalRef{id,revision+1}，clearedAt=nextMutationTime
  - expectCurrent：null→GOAL_NOT_FOUND 'no current goal'；ref 不匹配→GOAL_STALE_REVISION `stale goal ref "${id}" revision ${r}; current is "${id}" revision ${c}`
  - transitionError：`cannot ${op} goal "${id}" from phase "${phase}"; expected ${allowed.join(' or ')}`
- 时间：now=Date.now()；nextMutationTime=max(Date.now(), state.updatedAt)（防墙钟回拨）
- activation 机：runtimeStates WeakMap<Session,{activation,pendingActivation}>；agent/created→disarmed；session/event goal/change→pendingActivation offset 匹配则取之否则 disarmed；变更时 emit goal/activation-changed {sessionId, goal?{id,revision,activation}}
- commit：pendingActivation={offset:session.seq,activation}→session.append('goal/change',change)（append 后 offset 匹配则 activation 生效）→emit goal/changed（agentEvents scoped）{operation,ref,goal?:GoalView}
- GoalError extends HarnessError(message, code)；GOAL_CHANGE_VERSION=1

**tool-goal（index 343+authority 118+wrapup 41 全读）**：
- inject=['agents','goals','tools','systemPrompt','sessionProjections']；Config blockedAfterConsecutiveRounds 默认 3（正 safe int，TypeError 'blockedAfterConsecutiveRounds must be a positive safe integer'）
- section 'tool:goal' order=TOOL_GOAL text=guidance(threshold)：'create_goal may infer goal intent from a direct human request in any language. After session resume or fork, an active goal is disarmed: when a human asks to continue or resume in any wording or language, use update_goal action resume to rearm it. Mark complete only when the objective is actually achieved. Mark blocked only after the same blocking condition persists for at least ${blockedAfter} consecutive goal rounds, and report that concrete condition in blocked_reason; difficulty, uncertainty, or useful remaining work is not blocked.'（**动态文本**：阈值内嵌——catalog 抽取需参数化）
- goalToolExecution：agent 缺席→'goal tools require a calling agent' GOAL_TOOL_AGENT_REQUIRED；agents.get!==agent||status!=='running'||agents.currentInitiator()!==agent→'goal tools require the exact live calling agent inside its active driver' GOAL_TOOL_DRIVER_REQUIRED；turnBoundary 投影缺席或 openTurnStartSeq===null→'goal tools require an open model turn' GOAL_TOOL_DRIVER_REQUIRED；返回 {agent, events=snapshotEvents(), openTurnStartSeq}
- requireDirectHuman：roots().includes(agent) 且 open turn 内（seq openTurnStartSeq+1..end）存在 user/message 且 source.kind==='user'（followup/steer 无源默认 'user'=继承权限，非人生产者须自带源）；否则 reject 'this goal operation requires a direct human turn on a top-level agent' GOAL_TOOL_AUTHORITY_REQUIRED
- completionAuthority：direct-human 或 goal=view 且 open turn 内有 user/message source {kind:'goal',goalId,revision,round}===当前恰轮→{kind:'goal-round',goal}；否则 'complete and blocked require a direct human turn or the current goal round'
- goalRef 校验：goal_id 空/未 trim/revision 非正 safe int→'goal_id must be non-empty and revision must be a positive safe integer' GOAL_TOOL_INVALID_UPDATE
- get_goal：params {}；presentCall 'Read current goal' read
- create_goal：objective req（'The concrete completion objective inferred from the direct human request.'）+max_goal_rounds 可选（'Optional positive safe-integer limit on automatic continuation rounds.'）；requireDirectHuman；presentCall 'Create goal' other rawInput=objective
- update_goal：goal_id req/revision req/action req enum [edit,pause,resume,complete,blocked]/objective/max_goal_rounds/blocked_reason 可选；action 描述逐字（'edit, pause, and resume require a direct top-level human request. complete and blocked are also allowed during an automatic continuation of this goal; blocked is rejected before the configured minimum round count.'）
  - edit：requireDirectHuman；hasText(blocked_reason)→'blocked_reason is valid only with action blocked'
  - pause/resume：requireDirectHuman；objective/max_goal_rounds/blocked_reason 任一在场→'objective and max_goal_rounds are valid only with action edit; blocked_reason is valid only with action blocked'；resume 且 ref===current 且 phase==='paused'→'the model cannot resume a paused goal; the user must resume it' GOAL_TOOL_RESUME_PAUSED
  - complete/blocked（completionAuthority）：objective/max_goal_rounds 在场→'objective and max_goal_rounds are valid only with action edit'；complete+blocked_reason→'blocked_reason is valid only with action blocked'；blocked 缺/空 blocked_reason→'blocked_reason is required with action blocked'；blocked 且 goal-round 权且 roundsStarted<threshold→`blocked requires at least ${N} consecutive goal rounds; current round is ${roundsStarted}` GOAL_TOOL_BLOCK_THRESHOLD；block code='model-reported'
  - goal-round 权威时 deferContext(wrapup)：complete→<goal_complete> 块（objective JSON 化头+收尾指示+GROUNDING）；blocked→<goal_blocked> 块；source {kind:'tool-goal',form:'notice',summary=`${action}: ${objective}`}
- 输出 GOAL_VALUE_SCHEMA oneOf：{goal:null}|{goal{id,revision,objective,phase enum,roundsStarted,maxGoalRounds,blockedReason?{code,message}},activation enum armed|disarmed}；render=JSON.stringify(value)
- presentCall：`${action==='blocked'?'Mark':capitalize(action)} goal` other rawInput=blocked_reason||objective||max_goal_rounds||goal_id
- wrapup GROUNDING='Report only what earlier rounds and tool results in this session actually establish; when a detail is not in the session, say so instead of inventing it. '
- **M1 结构性缺席候选（待与 spec 场景对勘后呈 Q 裁决）**：agents 注册表（assertLive/roots/currentInitiator）、turnBoundary 投影 openTurnStartSeq、goal-round 消息源与 roundsStarted 推进（goal-round-driver 属 agent-loop 侧）、deferContext wrapup、通知机 wakeup/inject（jobs 完成通知同因）——M1 headless 单会话根 agent+每 turn 均直人输入→authority 恒 direct-human、通知机不可达；结构性缺席面如何落测须裁决

### 16c 域深读（续）：schedule 域全落（types 480+index 471+storage 60+update 179+runtime 166+delivery-history 77+domain 1896+tool 590 全读）

**词汇（types.ts）**：
- ScheduleId（branded）；六规则记录：after{afterSeconds,scheduledAt}/at{scheduledAt}/every{everySeconds≥60,scheduledAt}/daily{time HH:mm:ss.SSS 归一,timeZone,scheduledAt}/weekly{+weekdays ISO 1-7 唯一升序}/cron{expression 五字段 canonical,timeZone}；scheduledAt=四位年 RFC3339 UTC `.mmmZ` 串；title 必填≤120 trim 非空；prompt trim 非空
- ScheduleTask 行：{sessionId,record,status active|inactive(缺省 active),lastDelivery?,deliveryHistory?{records[{scheduledAt,deliveredAt,messageId,prompt?}],earlierRecordsUnavailable,earlierRecordsPruned?}}；校验 lastDelivery===最新 receipt
- ScheduleChange v1：create{schedule Legacy}/delete{id}/dispatch{id,acceptedAt?}（acceptedAt 仅 every）；schedule/change 会话事件
- 错误 union（**value 返回非抛**）：invalid_prompt/invalid_selector/invalid_rule/invalid_time_zone/not_future/time_out_of_range/frequency_too_high/subagent_session/internal_error；ScheduleInputError（服务层抛）vs ScheduleLogError（corrupt_schedule_log 持久层）
- ScheduleView=record+state scheduled|overdue+deliveryMode 'host'；ScheduleUpdateMiss{updated:false,code:schedule_not_found|schedule_ended|schedule_conflict}
- update CAS：expected 完整记录对比+timing change 可换 kind（at/every/daily/weekly/cron；after 不可改=需新建）
- delivery-history 分页：limit 1-100 safe int、before messageId 排他游标、nextBefore、earlierRecordsUnavailable/Pruned、retention 回显

**ScheduleService（index.ts 471）**：
- inject=['agents','sessions','storageDomain','sessionController','sessionPersistence']；Config deliveryHistoryDays 默认 30(1-3650)/deliveryHistoryRecords 默认 200(1-10000)
- 存储域 'schedule' v1 单表 tasks；open 时键≠record.id→`schedule: stored task key "${key}" differs from record id "${id}"`（先 close 再抛）
- serialize=FIFO promise 链；stopping→reject 'Schedule service is stopping'
- create：选择器>1→invalid_selector 'Exactly one reminder selector is required.'；title=scheduleTitle；id=`schedule-${randomUUID()}`；**now=队列入队前取样**（等待中可能已到期）；入队后 reminderTargetRefusal（agents.get(sessionId) 为委托子→subagent_session 'This Session belongs to subagent routing, which never receives reminder delivery.'）；行={sessionId,record,status:'active',deliveryHistory:{records:[],earlierRecordsUnavailable:false}}
- list：sessionId+active 过滤，存储序；catalog：全任务按 scheduledAt.localeCompare→id.localeCompare 排序；delete：get+sessionId 匹配否则 {deleted:false,code:'schedule_not_found'}；行删除含 deliveryHistory
- update：refusal→not_found(绑定不符)→ended(inactive)→resolveScheduleUpdate→put
- session/created 全局监听：fold 历史遗留事件，unreadable→warn `schedule: Session "${id}" historical events could not be read (${msg}); the legacy reminder is ignored.`；activeLegacy>0→warn `schedule: Session "${id}" contains legacy reminders; recreate active reminders with schedule_create.`
- 归档 admission：workspace/session-activity 增 {kind:'schedule',items:[{id,label:title}]}；workspace/session-stop→stopSessionTasks（队列内逐行删，聚合首错）
- emitChanged：schedule/changed emit（listener 异步失败吞 warn `schedule: schedule/changed listener failed: ...`）

**resolveScheduleUpdate（update.ts）**：expected decodeScheduleRecord 失败→{code:'invalid_rule',message:'expected must be a complete valid Schedule record.'}；deepEqual 失败→schedule_conflict；title=prompt 校验（retainedTitle 用 ScheduleLogError 面）；timing change validateChange（恰 kind+selector 两键）→等值归一（at 与 after|at 比 parseAtInput===Date.parse(scheduledAt)；every 同秒；daily/weekly 归一比对 canonicalizeTimeZone；cron canonical 表达式比对）→等值仅更 content；否则**以同一 now 重算目标**（recompute exactly as creation）

**ScheduleRuntime（runtime.ts）**：
- MAX_TIMER_DELAY_MS=2^31-1；单 timer+requested 标志+running promise；ctx.agents.withoutInitiator 驱动
- drive：scanNow 取 due（active+scheduledAt<=now）；recurring 按 sessionId 分组批量（同会话全部 recurring 成一 batch）；resolveAgent 恢复会话 agent；admitted=恢复后再滤（墙钟回滚防护）；recurring→resolveRecurringOccurrence 每 member+renderRecurringReminderBatchFraming；one-shot→renderReminderFraming；createUserMessage source{kind:'schedule'}；agent.followup(message)；**sessions.flush 确认持久**否则 throw 'Session persistence did not acknowledge the reminder'；one-shot→status inactive+appendDelivery；recurring→scheduledAt=nextScheduledAt??occurrenceAt、status 随之
- 失败：未 commit 且仍到期者入 failed 集（本次 drive 不再排 timer），warn `schedule: reminders ${JSON.stringify(ids)} were not acknowledged: ${err}`
- timer 重排：next=min(scheduledAt)，clamp [0,MAX_TIMER_DELAY_MS]，unref

**delivery-history.ts**：appendDelivery（floor=deliveredAt-days*86400s 过滤+尾 cap records；earlierRecordsUnavailable/Pruned 演进）；deliveryHistoryPage（before 游标 findIndex→-1=delivery_cursor_not_found；start=max(0,end-limit)；reverse 最新优先；start>0 且有 oldest→nextBefore）

**domain.ts（1896）核心算法**：
- 常量：SCHEDULE_CHANGE_VERSION=1/MIN_EVERY_INTERVAL_SECONDS=60/MAX_TITLE_LENGTH=120/CRON_SEARCH_HORIZON_YEARS=400（格里高利 400 年周期）；MIN/MAX_FOUR_DIGIT_YEAR_MS=0001-01-01T00:00:00.000Z/9999-12-31T23:59:59.999Z
- futureInstant：range 外→time_out_of_range 'The scheduled time must be representable as a four-digit-year RFC 3339 UTC instant.'；<=now→not_future 'The scheduled time must be strictly in the future.'
- parseOffsetInstant：OFFSET_INSTANT 正则（YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss[.ddd](Z|±HH:MM)）；-00:00 拒；hour>23 等拒→invalid_rule 'at must use YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss with optional 1-3 digit fractional seconds and an explicit Z or numeric offset.'
- canonicalizeTimeZone：空/未 trim/非 UTC 且非 IANA_ZONE 正则→invalid_time_zone 'time_zone must be UTC or a valid IANA Area/Location name.'；Intl resolvedOptions canonical 化（Rust=chrono-tz 名字归一）
- localInstant(Temporal disambiguation 'earlier')：重叠取早即时，gap→undefined（**chrono-tz LocalResult 映射：Ambiguous→earlier，None→gap，Single→值**）
- parseAtInput：串=offset 即时；对象恰 date,time,time_zone 三键→resolveLocalInstant（gap→invalid_rule 'The local at time does not exist in the selected time zone.'）；否则 'at must be an explicit-offset string or local calendar object.'
- 记录构造器六件（createXxxScheduleRecord）：prompt trim 非空→invalid_prompt 'prompt must be non-empty after trimming.'；scheduleTitle→invalid_prompt REQUIRED_TITLE_MESSAGE/'title must be at most 120 characters.'；after 正 safe int→invalid_rule 'after_seconds must be a positive safe integer.'；every safe int→'every_seconds must be a safe integer.'、<60→frequency_too_high 'every_seconds must be at least 60.'；daily/weekly/cron now 代表性→time_out_of_range 'Daily/Weekly/Cron creation time must be a representable four-digit-year UTC instant.'；无未来→'No future daily/weekly/cron occurrence is representable as a four-digit-year UTC instant.'
- 本地钟时间解析 LOCAL_CLOCK_TIME ^(?:[01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d:[0-5]\d(?:\.\d{1,3})?$→`${selector}.time must use HH:mm:ss with optional 1-3 fractional digits, without leap seconds or 24:00.`；存储侧要求 .SSS 归一（'daily/weekly time must be normalized to HH:mm:ss.SSS'）
- normalizeWeekdays：非空数组+1-7 整数+无重复→'weekly.weekdays must be a non-empty array of ISO weekday numbers.'/'Each weekly.weekdays entry must be an integer from 1 (Monday) through 7 (Sunday).'/'weekly.weekdays must not repeat weekday ${n}.'；存储侧升序校验 'weekly weekdays must be normalized to unique ascending ISO weekday numbers'
- decodeScheduleRecord：daily/weekly/cron 三 Host 形（恰键+title 必经 decodeStoredTitle：空/超长/未 trim→ScheduleLogError）+legacy after/at/every（title 可选，缺省不拒绝）；current Host 记录 legacy 形缺 title→REQUIRED_TITLE_MESSAGE
- 每次出现解析四件：resolveEveryOccurrence（steps=floor((acceptedAt-target)/interval) 不枚举积压；acceptedAt<target→'every dispatch cannot precede the active scheduledAt'）；daily/weekly=latestDueOccurrence（自 decision UTC 日期+1 向回走，max(candidate,savedTarget) 兜 tzdata 漂移）+next*Target（自 max(today,afterDate+1) 向前走，gap 日期跳过）；cron=latestCronOccurrence/nextCronTarget（400 年地平线+月跳跃+Vixie DOM/DOW_STAR 语义：**两侧均限=OR，任一侧星=AND**；day-of-week 7→0 折叠；canonical 编码 encodeCronField/encodeStarCronField 保 star 旗）
- decodeScheduleChange：version≠1→'schedule/change version must be 1'；三操作恰键；create schedule=legacy 形（title 可选）
- foldScheduleEvents/applyScheduleChanges：id 复用→`schedule id "${id}" was reused`；delete 非 active→`schedule delete targets inactive id "${id}"`；dispatch 非 active→同形；one-shot dispatch 带 acceptedAt→'one-shot dispatch must not contain acceptedAt'；every 无 acceptedAt→'every dispatch must contain acceptedAt'
- allocateScheduleId：schedule-N 首个未用 N（Host 存储不用——Host id=uuid；此为会话遗留 fold 面）
- scheduleView：state=now>=scheduledAt?'overdue':'scheduled'
- framing：'[SCHEDULE REMINDER]'+'This is a scheduled message from the user'+schedule_id_json/occurrence_at/reminder_prompt_json（JSON.stringify 转义）；批量='[SCHEDULE REMINDER BATCH]'+reminders_json:[{schedule_id,occurrence_at,reminder_prompt}]

**tool-schedule（590）**：见前述已 persist 要点+四工具参数 schema（SELECTOR_PARAMETERS 逐字 description）；schedule_delete id 校验在 agent 判定前；update 流=List 找 expected→catalog() 查 ended→schedule.update CAS；presentCall 'Create reminder'/'List reminders'/'Delete reminder'/'Update reminder'

**Rust 移植要点**：Temporal→chrono(+chrono-tz)；Intl.canonicalizeTimeZone→chrono_tz::Tz::from_str 归一（名字规范化）+UTC 特判；disambiguation 'earlier'→LocalResult::Ambiguous(_,later 用 earlier)/None=gap；MAX_TIMER_DELAY_MS 同值；四位数年界=chrono 显式范围检查；Date.parse/toISOString↔chrono RFC3339 with milliseconds Z 恒定格式

### 16c 批 0 裁决（2026-10-30 用户四裁决）+storage 栈真源

**裁决**：
- **Q16c-1A**：jobs 完成通知机本切片全落（截断梯+wakeBudget+quiet/wakeup+inject/followup；fr-agent-loop 'agent' 服务三方法为载体）——16a 遗留③就此关闭

- **Q16c-3B**：schedule 持久存储=**新 fr-storage crate**（generic domain/table 面独立插件；PRD 4.5 增行随裁决生效）
- **Q16c-4A**：IANA tz+日历=jiff（Temporal 语义直译：disambiguation earlier/reject 内建、ISO dayOfWeek 周一=1、内置 tzdb）
- 判例执行项（呈报无异议）：五 section 场景改否定（Q16b-5A 判例）；registry/subagent 拒绝臂在位+纯函数钉测+档载（16a 遗留①②同类处理）；fr-config 六组新键；fr-wire 三事件常量

**storage 栈真源（storage-domain 723+storage-json 481 全读）**：
- spec：DomainSpec{name(UNIT_NAME_RE),version(非负 int),layout single|per-record(缺省 single),compatibleVersions(旧版白名单,per-record 读放行/整单元拒),invalidRecords backup-and-skip(可弃派生数据；无 backupRecord 后端回退拒绝),global{schema,initial}(schema 禁 null=null 是"未写"哨兵),tables{name→{valueSchema}}}；defineDomain 模块载入即验（名/版本/layout/global-null）
- facility：单开表 reserved set（already-open）→backend 路由（routes 覆盖默认）→kv facet→open(descriptor{ name,version,tables,hasGlobal,layout,compatibleVersions })→loadAll→逐记录 schema 验（invalid-record 带 table/key；backup-and-skip 则移档+log error+跳过；global 恒拒绝）→DomainImpl；caller 持有 handle 自 close；facility 卸载关残留
- domain.ts 运行时：**权威态在内存**；每域单写链 FIFO（chain.then；link 恒 settle）；写序=后端耐久 FIRST→内存→emit domain/changed（listener 失败吞 warn `domain '${name}': domain/changed listener failed: ${err}`）；后端拒→内存不动（无读写分叉）；KvTable{get/entries/keys/size(内存快照),put(整记录无合并),delete(存在性在链槽判定,false 无写无事件),update(原子 RMW,缺键→missing-key)}；global{get(initial),set(首 set 才落盘)}；close: disposing 拒新写→drain 链→unit.close→closed 拒读→名字释放（幂等共享 teardown）
- storage-json：文件=<root>/<name>.json 恒为当前净态（pretty 2 空格+插入序+尾 \n；文档={unit:{name,version},global,tables}）；parse 错误=malformed-medium（非 JSON/非对象/头缺失或异名/tables 非对象或数组/表非对象）+version-mismatch（stored≠expected）；**atomic 协议=同目录 .{uuid}.tmp(wx 0o600)→fsync→rename 整替→POSIX fsync 目录**（win 跳过目录 fsync；session-log 的 link+unlink no-clobber 是另一协议，单元文件单写者 last-write-wins 正确）；single-unit：内存权威，每写原语变更后整文件重发布；发布失败回滚内存（put 恢复 previous/删除；delete/global 同）；close 恒 drain inFlight；写序归 caller（域层写链）——后端只保证单调用发布完整耐久文件
- 缺文件=空单元（物化延至首写）；M1 消费者 schedule=单表 tasks+无 global+single layout

### 16c 批 4 码面勘察（2026-10-31，实现前终勘）：jobs 三工具+完成通知机

**真源**：tool-jobs index.ts 416 行+render.ts 84 行全读。三工具=job_output（job_id req/wait/timeout_ms；wait→jobs.wait(min(timeout??30000,600000))；readBody=delta+result 接缝；output{text req,job req}；render=body(空→'(no new output)')+接缝+statusLine）+job_list（params{}；output 数组；render 空→'(no background jobs)'否则 `${id} [${kind}] ${status} — ${label}` join）+job_kill（job_id req/reason opt；kill→requested 加 killedByModel；get 投影；output{outcome enum[cancellation-requested,already-finished],job}；render 两态）。publicJob=剥 owner/offsets/limits 键序 id,kind,label,status,detail?,startedAt,finishedAt?；statusLine=detail?[status: S, D]:[status: S]；renderModelDelta=stdout 先+[stderr] 段+lossy/gap 尾注（spill paths join ', ' 或 '(unavailable)'）。

**完成通知机（index.ts:261-309）**：killedByModel Set+events.subscribe({owners:'scope'})；settled 臂：removed→set.delete；delivered=killedByModel.delete(id)||event.awaited；delivered||cause==='teardown'||owner undefined→return；owner=agents.get()；message=createUserMessage{text:fitCompletionNotice(job), source:{kind:'tool-jobs',form:'notice',summary:completionSummary}}；wakeup&&owner.status==='idle'→（无预算→followup；spent<cap→+1+followup）否则 inject。fitCompletionNotice 梯：complete=`background job ${id}`+` (${kind}: ${label}) finished ${statusLine}`+'. Read its output with job_output.'→超帽 fixed=prefix+'\n[notice truncated]'+'\nDone; job_output.'→再超 compact=prefix+action→再超 recovery='background job ended; use job_list for id.'→retainHead('job_list',cap)。fitWithSuffix=complete→(content 已尾 omitted.trimStart()?''：omitted)+suffix（fixedBytes>=cap→retainTail(fixed)；否则 retainTail(content,cap-fixed)+fixed）。completionSummary=boundContextSummary(`${kind} ${label} ${statusLine}`)；boundContextSummary=120 UTF-16 单位帽（>120→slice(0,119)+'…'）。

**finalizeContent（:220-245）**：tools/pre-execute prepend 记 visibleOutputLimit（job_output/job_kill 按 arguments.job_id 查 list）；finalize 臂：job_output 非 error 且 rawSingleText(content)===默认渲染→fitWithSuffix(body 去尾 \n, '\n'+statusLine, cap, '\n[output truncated]')；否则 boundSingleText '\n[result truncated]'（content 非恰一 text 块→undefined 原样）。

**fr 载体（Q16c-1A 执行）**：①fr-shell settle() 发内核事件 "job/settled" 载 dsh hub 形状 {type:'settled',job:view,cause,awaited}（fr-llm emit 判例；C6 不取发射方锁；lane 队列异步派发→无 reentrancy 环）；awaited=每 job 等待者计数（wait() 首个 terminal 检查失败后计数 RAII 增减；settle 锁内快照）。②fr-tools-builtin descriptor listen {"job/settled":1,"agent/inbox/claimed":2}（listen map 值=事件 id u32，fr-agent-loop EV_CHUNK_READY=1 判例）；on_event 快路径（AGENT-LOOP spec :27 禁 require 纪律）：settled→killedByModel Mutex set 判+构造 Message→mpsc 入队；claimed→source kind user 且 budget Some→重置。③通知 worker 线程（BuiltinState 自有 plugin-owned 线程，teardown join，空 require 栈无环）：recv→require 'agent' status 6→idle&&wakeup→预算臂→followup 3 else inject 2；quiet 恒 inject。④授权=fr-agent-loop setup grant("builtin-tools","agent")（Outbound 增 grant 法；缺席=STATUS_UNDECLARED 立即失败=干净跳过，16a watcher 判例；装配序要求 builtin-tools 先于 agent-loop 提交=fr-shell grant("tools",…) 同族契约）。⑤killedByModel 竞速修正：job_kill 体先验 add（dsh 单线程 add-after-kill 的跨插件竞速不可复制；'already-finished'/Err 移除）——可观察契约（模型自杀不重复通知）保持。⑥finalizeContent=fr post-execute CPS 监听器两件（METHOD 111/112，spill 判例：enter 停靠 delegate/exit 换 accept content）；visibleOutputLimit 现场 list 查询（dsh WeakMap 快照臂=M1 不可达：settled job 留表 outputLimitBytes 不变，档载）。⑦InboxClaimedPayload 增 session_id（loop_inbox.claim 处 client.session_id() 在手；dsh 载荷携 Agent 对象引用，fr 诚实序列化）。

**M1 档载（批 0 判例族，无新裁决）**：removed 清理臂（fr 无内核 removed 事件→set 清理骑 settled 送达，条目寿命≤进程）；owner 缺席臂（owner=caller 会话，teardown→cause teardown 跳过；worker require 失败=同形跳过）；status 查询→投递 TOCTOU（跨插件异步 vs dsh 单线程原子；followup 恒带 wake=idle 臂安全，inject 臂窄窗=档载）；装配校验两文案（waitDefault>waitCap/maxConsecutiveWakes 非整数）=纯函数+合成钉测（goal resolveConfig 判例，baked 值恒过）；claimed 监听器无条件声明+budget None no-op（dsh 条件注册无观察差）。

### 16c 批 5 码面勘察（2026-10-31，实现前终勘）：schedule 域全件

- **tool-schedule 不注册 section**（apply 只注册四工具）→批 0 已裁「五 section 场景改否定」批 5 执行；sections 钉测保持 9 条+schedule 缺席断言。

- dsh runtime.ts:122 确证 followup **同步追加** inbox splice 后 flush 观察；fr followup=信箱异步（NextTurn+wake）→投递确认臂= followup→require session-persistence flush（METHOD_SESSION_FLUSH=4）→Err='Session persistence did not acknowledge the reminder'；消息落日志时序差=批 4 TOCTOU 档载同族。
- resolveAgent 会话恢复臂：fr 无 session-controller 服务，M1 投递不恢复死会话（require 失败=owner 缺席臂族）；恢复后重滤 admitted（墙钟回滚防护）随恢复面缺席同档载——M1 admitted=group 原样。

Narrow the query or add filters to find additional matches.'
- 呈现：formatSessionSearch 空表='No prior session matches found.'；行模板 1. Session id — title/Created/Parent(root|id|[outside workspace])/Availability/Best match seq|type|surface|time/Snippet；event_search 行=seq|type|surface|time+Snippet；session_trace=Ancestors (nearest first)+[outside workspace boundary]+Descendants 树缩进两空格；event_trace 七行（Target/Replaced by/Replacement chain/Events replaced by target/Events cited directly as sources/Direct derived events，空='none'）；event_read=```json JSON.stringify(target,null,2)```+Before/After 邻居（extractSessionEventText，空='(no semantic text)'）；时间=toISOString
- 呈现卡：presentSessionSearchCall{card:'generic',kind:'search',title:'Search prior sessions',rawInput:query}；event search 同形 title 'Search session events'；trace title=含 session_id 前缀两态；event target 卡 title=`${action} ${seq}`（'Read event'/'Trace event'）rawInput={session_id?,seq}
- service-boundary 安全翻译表 SAFE_SESSION_QUERY_FAILURES 16 码（SEARCH_DISABLED→'session search is disabled in this deployment'；INVALID_CONFIG/SOURCE_CONFLICT→吞成 SESSION_QUERY_TOOL_FAILED 'session query operation failed'；其余各码定文案）；未列码/非 SessionQueryError→generic；logger.warn 全诊断
- workspaceAccess：readTitles 经 readTitleSnapshots（rejected→untitled+unavailableCode 呈现 `title (title unavailable: ${code})`，UNAUTHORIZED 上抛）；authorizeDescendants/visitDescendants 不授权子树=[outside workspace subtree]；titleText 拼接规则

**session-query 服务核心（12 文件 ~2140 行全读：types 290/index 408/config 57/corpus 323/observation 367/tracing 254/filters 243/documents 75/extraction 87/sources 25/cursor 15/cold-read 57）**：
- SessionQueryEngine 抽象基类 inject=['sessions']；Config{readWindowMax 默认 50 非负 int/persistedReadConcurrency 默认 4 正 safe int/preparedSessionCacheSize 默认 5 正 safe int}；三校验文案 'session-query: readWindowMax must be a non-negative integer' 等
- 具体方法（backend 无关）：observeSession→observation reader；listSessions（live-preferred corpus newest-first：persisted.list 为底+live sessions 覆盖+assertSessionHeadersCompatible 冲突拒；排序 createdAt desc+id localeCompare）；readSession（Session.create 回放校验后 structuredClone 快照）；filterSessions（materialize 校验+谓词过滤）；readTitle/readTitleSnapshot/readTitleSnapshots（foldSessionTitle per-session 批投影，projectMany 有界并发 persistedReadConcurrency，rejected 隔离/取消全灭）；listEvents（tracing.eventRecords）；filterEvents（buildSessionEventSearchDocuments+谓词）；readSurface（foldSurface current 面）；traceSession（ancestors 链上溯+环检测 SESSION_QUERY_INVALID_LINEAGE `session lineage contains a cycle at "${id}"`+descendants 树 childrenByParent createdAt 升序构建；complete/root 或 complete:false/unresolvedParentId）；traceEvent（analyzeEventLog 单次 fold：replacedBy 链/replacedEventSeqs/sourceEventSeqs=event.sourceEventSeqs/derivedEventSeqs=后续引用者）；readEvent（before/after 窗 `must be an integer between 0 and ${readWindowMax}` SESSION_QUERY_INVALID_WINDOW；EVENT_NOT_FOUND `session "${id}" has no event at seq ${seq}`）
- 抽象方法（backend 实现）：searchSessions/searchEvents——session-query-sqlite 是唯一 backend；**fr M1 对应=SEARCH_DISABLED 终态**（base openAt:never 语义）
- corpus：可选 sessionPersistence 绑定（fiber 注入+effect 清理）；load live 优先（live 不落 persistence；挂载竞态三重检查）；notFound `session "${id}" not found`；persistence 错误映射 PERSISTENCE_FAILED `session persistence listing failed: ${msg}`/CORRUPT_SESSION `stored session "${id}" is corrupt: ${msg}`；SessionPersistenceCorruptionError 按名映射
- cold-read：open('read')→read(0,undefined)→close→events+interruptedTurnClosers(events)（fr-session 切片 F/G 等价物已在）
- observation：SessionObservation lease（live/prepared 双源+retain/Symbol.dispose+refs pin+LRU 容量 cacheSize+revision 键 statSource 先 stat 后重查 live 三重竞态防护；prepare 失败且 live 出现→continue 重试；projectionMode all/none；prepared projections=hydrate+可选 cache）
- sources.assertSessionHeadersCompatible 五字段（id/createdAt/cwd/parentSession/isSeeded/delegationDepth??0）→SOURCE_CONFLICT `session source headers conflict for session "${id}"`
- extraction.extractSessionEventText：user/message+assistant/message 内容文本（reasoning 块排除）/tool/call=name+arguments/tool/result=content+error.name+error.code/todo/write=status+content/turn/end reason（error→'error'+message、aborted、max-tokens、interrupted；completed→''）；joinText=trim+filter(Boolean)+\n
- documents.buildSessionEventSearchDocuments：foldSurface 分类 current/shadowed/log-only（foldSurface 错→INVALID_SURFACE `invalid session surface: ${msg}`）+非空文本文档
- filters：materialize 校验（值类型/allowed values/safe range 文案族 `session ${name} filter ${detail}`）；compileSessionTextFilter=字面量转义+空白折叠 \s++'iu' 旗（空文本 INVALID_FILTER 'session text filter must contain non-whitespace text'）
- 错误码闭集 17（SessionQueryErrorCode，config.ts）
- fr 载体注：sessions.get/list→fr-agent-loop 域（M1 单会话：corpus live 面=当前会话；跨会话 live 面 M1 无=Phase 18 注册表）；sessionPersistence require 面（stat/list/open/read 全在 fr-session persist 服务）；Q110′A′ 继电已备 tail-pull 判例

**CONFIG spec 16d 冻结场景（22 条，键全在）**：dsh-plan-mode section 2（string，plan 活动期渲染 plan:policy 段）；dsh-session-query-sqlite 12（journalMode 'wal'/defaultLimit 20/maxLimit/snippetChars/persistedReadConcurrency/preparedSessionCacheSize 各默认+约束）；dsh-tool-ask-user 4（mode 'legacy'|'timed' 默认 legacy/timeout 120s）；dsh-tool-session-query 4（maxSearchResults 100/searchTimeoutMs 30000）。user-questions Config=z.object({}) 无键无场景。fr-schema registry=fr-tools-builtin 平铺增 16d 组（16c 批 2 判例），session-query 核心 readWindowMax 键无 CONFIG 场景（仅 sqlite 组带并发/缓存两键——sqlite Config extends core Config 的平铺结果，注册表按 CONFIG spec 场景键落）

**TOOLS spec 16d 冻结场景 47 条**：ask 6/exit 6/五件 session 工具各 7（调度与呈现/越界拒绝/section/query 形状/query 约束/行为 1……session_search 8 条含 availability 形状+约束）。行为场景冻结文本：ask=「默认阻塞等回答；config mode:timed 启用超时+pending result（timeout 默认 120s，-1 无限阻塞）」；exit=「schema 常驻防 churn；plan 外 execute 拒绝；plan 内经 user-questions 呈批 approve/继续规划；批准在 step 边界记录 inactive」；session_search=「跨 workspace 搜先前会话每会话回最强匹配；config maxSearchResults 100、searchTimeoutMs 30000」
